  "content": "[HANDOFF] 你是 Main Agent，这是 Loop #8。\n\n## 上一轮交接 (task_context)\n\n### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线\n- GPU 白名单：仅 `westd-11235:cuda:0` 和 `westd-11235:cuda:1`，config 里只有这两张卡\n- DeepSeek 组实验必须用旧代码（`load_best_model_at_end` 已注释为 `# TEMP_DISABLED`），保持 DeepSeek 组内 checkpoint selection 一致性（都用 epoch 3 final）。Qwen 组可用新代码\n- `deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v3` 必须用 `to_chat_text()` label-preserving truncation 修复版代码，不能用旧截断逻辑（v1/v2 坍塌根因就是 DeepSeek tokenizer 产生 >2048 tokens → 右截断 labels）\n- Qwen exception_only 99.83% 是**表征理论上限**（irreducible error = 2 个 `GeneratorExit` 样本 id=901/905），不要试图提升\n- Bootstrap 方法：论文同一表格不能混 paired/unpaired。DeepSeek VOTA 和 no_trace 缺 per-sample predictions，需要补跑 `evaluate.py --save_predictions` 后 paired bootstrap\n\n### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照\n- **服务器**: westd-11235, 2x RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 94GB, CUDA 12.8\n- **工作目录**: `/root/trace-verdict-study`\n- **Python**: `/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base 环境，不是 venv）\n- **模型**: Qwen = HF cache, DeepSeek = `/root/autodl-tmp/models/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct/deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6___7b-instruct`\n- **数据**: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-data/` (train 4720, test 1190)\n- **Checkpoints**: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/`\n- **HF_HOME**: `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache`\n- **W&B project**: `trace-verdict-study`\n- **活跃进程**:\n  - `deepseek_exception_only_seed456`: PID 160932, cuda:0, 启动 ~20:19 UTC, 预计 ~2h\n  - `deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v3`: PID 163109, cuda:0, 启动 ~20:55 UTC, 预计 ~2h\n  - ⚠️ 两个进程可能都分配到 cuda:0——需首先 SSH 检查 `nvidia-smi` 确认是否 GPU 冲突\n- **Cron**: 2 个活跃 cron 监控上述两个实验\n\n### 3. 📊 当前状态\n我在本 session 中处理了大量实验回调和 Director 指令：D019-D028 全部在今天产生。核心事件链：\n\n1. 跨模型验证完成（D019）→ exception signal universal 0pp gap 叙事建立\n2. DeepSeek seed123 negative（91.09%）→ 0pp gap 叙事崩塌（D025-D028）\n3. Epoch1 hypothesis 被推翻（seed123 epoch1=91.43%，从未接近 99%）→ Claim 1 major revision\n4. seed456 紧急排期（cuda:0）+ full_trace v3 提交（label truncation fix）\n5. vota_seed456_v2 刚完成：99.08%，VOTA seed robustness 确认\n6. paper_framing.md 更新至 v2.8\n\n**Registry RUNNING**:\n- `deepseek_exception_only_seed456` — 决定 Claim 1 最终 framing 的关键实验（3-seed 方差量化）\n- `deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v3` — DeepSeek 4/4 条件的最后一个，label truncation 修复后重训\n\n**等待中**:\n- 上述两个实验的 cron 完成回调\n- seed456 完成后需向 Director 汇报 Claim 1 最终 framing 决策\n- Paper Agent 的 Claim 1 相关章节暂停中，等 seed456 数据\n\n### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）\n- `deepseek_exception_only_seed456` cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 eval → 3-seed 方差计算 → 如果 seed456 ≥ 95% → Claim 1 framing: \"two modes: stable high (seed42) vs LoRA-sensitive (seed123)\"; 如果 seed456 ≈ 91% → framing: \"consistently best but model-dependent ceiling with high variance\"; 如果 seed456 ≈ 99% → seed123 是 outlier，framing 更乐观 → 无论哪种，汇报 Director 确定最终 Claim 1 措辞\n- `deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v3` cron 回调 → eval → 填充跨模型对比表最后一格（DeepSeek full_trace）→ 如果 >50% 说明 truncation fix 有效，与 Qwen 81.8% 对比；如果仍坍塌 → 分析 truncation 是否真的修复了\n- 两个实验都完成后 → spawn worker 为 DeepSeek VOTA 和 no_trace 补跑 `evaluate.py --save_predictions` + paired bootstrap CI → 更新 registry metrics\n- Claim 1 framing 确定后 → 通知 Paper Agent 恢复 Claim 1 相关 12+ 处修改 → paper_framing.md finalize\n- GPU 空闲后 → 如有时间，plan_005 第二任务或额外 ablation\n\n### 5. 🎯 战略上下文\n- **DDL**: ARR May 25 (10 天)。10 天冲刺路线在 ROADMAP.md 中\n- **核心叙事转折**: D028 标志着从 \"exception signal is model-agnostic universal (0pp gap)\" 转向 \"exception is consistently the best component, but absolute ceiling is model-dependent\"。这是本项目最大的 framing 调整，seed456 是最后的数据点\n- **决策链**: D019(universal) → D025(seed123 negative) → D026(epoch hypothesis) → D027(epoch rejected) → D028(major revision)。下一个\"我\"必须理解这条链，不要试图恢复 \"0pp gap\" 叙事\n- **易错点**:\n  - 不要把 DeepSeek seed42 epoch1 (99.83%) 当作 \"DeepSeek 也能 99%\"——seed123 证明这不稳定\n  - 不要混淆 epoch1 和 epoch3 (final) 的 accuracy——DeepSeek seed42 epoch1=99.83% 但 final=99.33%\n  - GC4-par vs GC4-seq 类比：不同 seed 的绝对值不能直接对比，要看相对于同 seed 同模型 baseline 的 lift\n  - vota_seed456_v2 (99.08%) 结果刚出，还没向 Director 正式汇报（仅更新了 registry），下一个 session 应补汇报\n  - 两个 RUNNING 实验可能存在 cuda:0 冲突，必须第一时间 `nvidia-smi` 确认\n\n## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)\n\n## 环境信息 [2026-05-14]\n- westd-11235: 2x RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 94GB, CUDA 12.8\n- 模型通过 ModelScope CDN 下载\n- \"pass\"/\"fail\" 在 Qwen/DeepSeek tokenizer 中均为单 token\n- Qwen2.5-Coder chat template overhead: 31 tokens\n\n## 可查的历史\n前 30 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。\n完整聊天归档可用 `recall_session_history(\"关键词\")` 搜索。\n\n## 第一个动作要求\n1. 用 `read_registry` 确认当前 RUNNING/PREPARING 实验状态（防止幻觉记忆过时状态）\n2. 回复一句话确认你看到了上面的硬约束 + 当前活跃实验，再开始任何工具调用"

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

  "task_type": "ops:gpu-check",

  "task_prompt": "紧急任务：检查 westd-11235 上的 GPU 状态。\n\n**背景**：当前有两个训练实验同时运行，都声称使用 --gpu 0：\n1. `deepseek_exception_only_seed456` (PID 160932)\n2. `deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v3` (PID 163109)\n\n需要立即确认是否存在 GPU 冲突。\n\n**操作步骤**（全部用 ssh_read_only）：\n\n1. `nvidia-smi` — 查看 GPU 显存使用和进程\n2. `ps aux | grep -E '160932|163109' | grep -v grep` — 确认两个 PID 是否存活\n3. `cat /proc/160932/environ 2>/dev/null | tr '\\0' '\\n' | grep CUDA` — 查看 PID 160932 的 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\n4. `cat /proc/163109/environ 2>/dev/null | tr '\\0' '\\n' | grep CUDA` — 查看 PID 163109 的 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\n\n**服务器**：westd-11235\n**环境**：conda base, /root/miniconda3/bin/python\n\n**产出**：报告以下信息：\n- 每张 GPU 的显存使用情况\n- 每个 PID 实际使用的物理 GPU\n- 是否存在 GPU 冲突（两个进程在同一物理 GPU）\n- 如果有冲突，两个进程的训练进度（查看日志最后几行：`tail -5 /root/runs/trace-verdict-study/deepseek_exception_only_seed456.log` 和类似路径）",
